# AI/ML day-by-day schedule

Generated from ai-ml-weekly-syllabus.csv by scripts/ai-study.ps1 -Action Build. Read ai-ml-learning-contract.md first. Day numbers are study units, not calendar deadlines. Six study units plus one rest unit per week.

## Day 1 - Week 1 - Python and the ML workflow: First principles

- **Study:** Features targets training inference and evaluation
- **Required output:** Work one concrete example by hand and write an explanation in your own words.
- **Prompt:** How does learning a model differ from writing rules?
- **Past-to-present link:** . Explain one connection before introducing new material; do not assume prior mastery.
- **Recall:** use actual due reviews, oldest/high-priority first, usually at most three in 10-20 minutes. Keep unanswered reviews pending.
- **Checkpoint:** record the learner's answer, evidence, tests where executable work is required, relevant rubric scores, corrections, gaps, and exact next task. Rest days have no new-output requirement.
